A blindfold worn before exposure preserves adaptation and prevents the dazzle and retinal injury.

Begin {} course
Being cold raises your blood pressure directly, and it is the cause people never guess because nothing about a cold body says headache. Three degrees under is worth several points of pressure on its own, which is often the whole difference between comfortable and pounding. Warm up first, before treating anything.
Being properly warm improves clotting slightly. Pressure held on the wound helps while it is held. Nothing beats closing it.
Being soaked removes the protection your clothing was giving you. Drenched on a freezing peak is not the same situation as dry on one, and it is the usual reason someone freezes while wearing a coat.
Below a critical systolic pressure, consciousness is capped at zero outright. Nothing else you do matters until the pressure comes up.
Below the requirement - the result may be shoddy, or the blade may bite.

Brittle Bones
Broad cyst-jungle rarity. The world is divided into deterministic 8x8-chunk underground regions; one region in this many is selected. 1 makes every eligible region cyst growth, 16 is the rare authored default, and 128 is extremely rare. Forge keeps whatever value an existing world's server config already saved, so a world made under an earlier version keeps its old rarity until you set this one by hand.

MicrobialLoad

Only matters when vanillaHealingBridge is on. false (default) = healing tops your blood up to a normal 5 litres and stops there. true = it keeps pushing past full, toward the 7.5 litre ceiling. Being over-full is not free: it raises blood pressure by up to about a quarter, which brings the hypertensive headache and, sustained, the stroke risk above 180 systolic. Repeated healing - a lifesteal enchantment, say - will get you there on its own. Turn this on if you want that as a hazard to manage rather than something the body simply refuses.

Partial-save the alive-timer on death. 0 (default) = the timer FULLY resets to 0 when you die - the intended hardcore behaviour. N > 0 = instead it drops to the last completed N-hour checkpoint, so a death costs at most N hours (e.g. 1 = saves every hour: die at 6h30 -> back to 6h). The hack stays permanent once earned either way.

Saline raises volume without adding red cells. It will hold your pressure up, which buys real time, but it cannot carry oxygen - a saline-filled body can have a decent pressure and still be starving.

Turning and supporting the head
Two routes. Environmental: actual suspended geology dust or organic bioaerosols retained in poorly exchanged deep air, breathed while your immunity is already low. And person to person, airborne, with risk falling over distance - indoors is worse. Darkness and depth alone cannot create contamination. Ventilation clears the environmental route; a fitted respirator or rebreather suppresses what reaches the airway and spends filter while doing so.

Venom
Venom in the blood · it stops your wounds clotting · antivenom.
Venom is an anticoagulant. It scales clotting down on a curve and reaches ZERO at a high enough dose - a venomous bite does not merely hurt, it stops your wounds closing while you bleed from them.

When ANY player says doom/doomed in chat, flash an Invincible-style DOOMED title card + fanfare on every Doomed player's screen. A gag - on by default.
When moodles are right-anchored, use an optional <name>_right.png from resource packs. If it is absent, mirror the normal art so decorative extensions point inward. Disable to draw the same orientation on both sides.
When true, WOUNDS survive death: torn skin, wasted muscle, fractures and their splints carry to the new body. Lethal state never carries - blood, brain, bleeding, infection and organs always reset, so a respawn is never a death loop. Off by default: Doomed's death already costs everything. Turn it on if you want the body you earned to follow you through the respawn.
When true, a head-first landing is lethal outright - the brain is destroyed rather than concussed. Death in this mod is brain death, so this routes through the normal death path, not an instant-kill. Off by default.

Whether ordinary chat appears over the speaker when allowChatPresentation is false.

Whether the vanilla hearts show when allowVanillaHearts is false.
Which body sprite set the status panel's paper doll uses (cosmetic only - it changes no limb, wound or gameplay). DOOMED is the shipped Matu art. HUMAN is the human set and ALT is the furry set; both read their sprites from textures/gui/body/human/ and /alt/ and fall back to DOOMED when that art is absent, so selecting an empty slot can never render a missing-texture doll. Resource packs can also override the sprites directly without using this at all.
Which creatures a plant may seize, when grabberGrabsMobs is true. EMPTY (default) = any hostile monster. Otherwise ONLY the entity ids listed here, hostile or not - e.g. ["minecraft:zombie", "minecraft:skeleton"] for a plant that eats the shambling ones and ignores everything else. Ids are "modid:entity"; unknown ids are ignored rather than crashing, so a list can outlive the mod that needed it.
Which look the Doomed screens use. DIAGNOSTIC (default) is the cyan clinical readout, with its pale scan grid and thin bordered panels. CLASSIC is the darker, plainer interface it replaced. Presentation only - the same readings are on both.

false (default) = at mood -50 or lower, apathy hides hunger/thirst moodle chips and the optional hotbar gauges without changing either underlying need. true is an explicit presentation override: those channels stay visible, with gauges dimmed.
false (default) = at mood -{} or lower, apathy hides hunger/thirst moodle chips and the optional hotbar gauges without changing either underlying need. true is an explicit presentation override: those channels stay visible, with gauges dimmed.
false (default) = grabber plants only ever seize PLAYERS. true = they also seize creatures that wander into reach, thrash them and throw them clear, exactly as they do to you. Bosses are NEVER grabbed whatever you put here - a plant is not going to pick up the wither - and neither are other grabber plants' victims, tamed pets, or anything too big to lift. Narrow it further with grabberMobWhitelist below.
false (default) = no flavor chat message for routine medical actions (bandaging, splinting, disinfecting, etc.); the health panel and moodles already show the result. true = restore the narration.
false (default) = player deaths never create a colonized corpse carrier. true opts the server into a separate hostile carrier entity after a player dies on cyst substrate; it never takes ownership of, deletes or modifies another mod's protected player corpse.
false = a server-policy kill-switch on the entire opioid family, and it now means what it says: they do not appear ANYWHERE. Every administration path is inert and levels already in a body clear out; the items are gone from loot tables, trader shelves, worldgen fluid barrels and every creative tab; wild opium poppies stop generating and a surviving poppy stops dropping anything; and the whole synthesis chain that turns poppies into morphine, heroin and fentanyl is removed from the machines and from JEI/EMI, including the now-irrelevant naloxone chain and supplies. Withdrawal and antagonist decay still run so nobody is left frozen mid-state. Turn this off if opioid use is something your server would rather not simulate at all.
false = chemistry-machine fumes no longer harm you: standing near a running kiln/still/reactor without a mask stops causing sickness/nausea/toxic chest injury. The machines still run and the other hazards (fire, overheat, explosions) are unaffected.
false = contact traps (spike trap, bear trap, sawblade, caltrops, gunmine, coil, sound cannon, grabber plant) inflict no body-sim wounds - they still trigger visually.
false = corpse organ harvesting is off: violent deaths stop scattering the cadaver torso and the bone-saw extraction refuses. The surgeon discipline (INT-gated, freshness-clocked, donor-capped, witnessed, one harvest per donor per 30 min) is grim by design - servers that want transplants to stay loot-only turn this off. Requires enableOrgans. Note: skillsResetOnDeath=true (the default) is ALSO load-bearing here - it is what stops a lone surgeon farming their own respawned corpses, since dying costs them the INT the saw demands.

false = hazard TRAPS (spike/bear traps, mines, live wires, sound cannons, grabber plants...) no longer generate in NEWLY generated chunks. Traps already in your world stay, and beneficial scatter (crates, plants, resources) is unaffected.
false = internal-organ physiology is off. The liver cascade (sustained painkiller / radiation / sepsis -> toxaemia + jaundice -> hepatic encephalopathy) and the kidney cascade (dehydration / radiation / sepsis -> hyperkalaemia -> heart-rhythm collapse) both stop, and every organ RESTORES to whole - potassium and jaundice are read by the heart and the skin ungated, so a frozen kidney would be an untreatable heart attack. Leave on for the full sim.

triggered
true (default) = cyst eyes sweep their spotlights, are pulled toward a carried light, and report a player they catch to nearby Deep Maws. false leaves them standing as inert scenery - they stop sweeping, stop lighting the cave and stop telling anything where you are.
true (default) = cyst territory runs its bounded biological lifecycle: exposed larvae, interruptible dormant corpses, motile carriers and finite rooted brood nodes. false keeps the complete biome palette and water pods but removes this hostile ecology.
true (default) = each generated cyst grove remembers server-confirmed disturbance. Being seen, heard, breaking tissue, shooting lenses, mining and starting nearby fires raise a hidden shared value that only speeds the eyes' ordinary sweep. It never widens their cone, bypasses cover, changes calm green beams to yellow, or directly alerts a Maw.
true (default) = grabber plants (the 'snake grabbers' that seize and thrash you) generate on the surface in new chunks. false = none generate - independent of the trap toggle, so you can keep the mines/traps and lose only the grabbers, or vice versa.
true (default) = one player can amputate another. A limb that is already lost (necrotic, gangrenous or destroyed) can be taken from someone who cannot answer, because that is the emergency the feature exists for. A limb that could still be saved needs the patient awake and consenting, so nobody loses a working arm while asleep or AFK. false removes the interaction entirely.
true (default) = prolonged breathing near authored cyst spore blooms builds a hidden, recoverable particulate burden. Respiratory protection slows it sharply; only sustained unprotected exposure can seed the existing treatable Marrow Worm pathway. This is gameplay-only and independent of the client's spore/fog visual settings.
true (default) = sparse GLOWCAP survival flora generates on cave floors - a renewable food + bandage source underground so caving isn't a dead end. It glows faintly. Set false if you don't want any glowy plants in your caves.
true (default) = the once-per-life LAST STAND second wind can fire at the brink - the 'I won't give up yet' cutscene that sometimes pulls you back from brain death. false = no last stand and no cutscene; the brink is final. Turn off if you don't want the reprieve, or if the cutscene conflicts with another mod's death/voice handling.
true (default) = trader lair structures (the maid / adventurer vendor rooms) generate underground in new chunks. false = they no longer generate.
true (default) = water scooped straight out of a lake with a glass bottle is as murky as water cupped in your hands, and wants boiling. false = glass bottles sanitise on contact, which is what they used to do: cupping your hands in a pond risked sickness while dipping a bottle in the SAME pond was clean and hydrated you better.
true (default) = what you eat and drink moves your core temperature: a hot meal warms you, cold water and chilled fruit cool you. false = food is nutrition only.
true (default) = your Strength/Resilience/Intelligence skill levels reset to baseline when you die, so death costs your training. false = skills persist across death (the old behaviour). Does NOT apply to /doomedheal.
true = a new Doomed character is asked which run they want, once, before the ritual.
true = a running antibiotic course actively drives infection back down on every infected limb (about -4%/min net, so a 10-minute oral course knocks roughly 40% off and a bad infection takes two). This is the realistic behaviour: antibiotics kill the bacteria rather than only helping you cope. It stays SLOWER than a topical antiseptic on the wound itself, so source control still matters. false = the old model where antibiotics only raised immunity and could essentially never reverse an infection.
true = a running antibiotic course actively drives infection back down on every infected limb (about -{}%/min net, so a {}-minute oral course knocks roughly {}% off and a bad infection takes two). This is the realistic behaviour: antibiotics kill the bacteria rather than only helping you cope. It stays SLOWER than a topical antiseptic on the wound itself, so source control still matters. false = the old model where antibiotics only raised immunity and could essentially never reverse an infection.
true = advanced illness can make your on-screen vitals show WRONG (but plausible)
true = an EXTREME unarmored hit (a .50 BMG-class round or comparably destructive force) GIBS the struck region: a bare head or torso is instant death (the body comes apart), a bare arm/leg is torn off (survivable, but the stump bleeds hard). Ordinary rounds (pistols, SMGs, rifles, arrows) never trigger it. false = those hits wound normally.
true = an EXTREME unarmored hit (a .{} BMG-class round or comparably destructive force) GIBS the struck region: a bare head or torso is instant death (the body comes apart), a bare arm/leg is torn off (survivable, but the stump bleeds hard). Ordinary rounds (pistols, SMGs, rifles, arrows) never trigger it. false = those hits wound normally.
true = blood bags age and eventually become unsafe to transfuse. false = their stored condition remains fresh; food spoilage is a separate switch.

true = vanilla/modded HEALING (golden apples, Regeneration, healing spells - anything that fires a heal) reaches the body sim: it clots bleeding wounds (shrapnel still holds them open), closes skin and muscle on the worst limb, and per scope restores blood or chips infection. It NEVER touches brain damage, set fractures or dislocations - those always want real treatment. Off by default: Doomed healing is the point of Doomed.
true = when a player types doom in chat, a DOOMED card slams onto every Doomed player's screen with a fanfare. It is a gag and it is on by default. Turn it off on a public server: anyone can type the word, so anyone can put a banner across everyone else's view. Each player can also switch it off for themselves in the client settings; this is the switch they cannot ignore.
true = your main inventory grid is limited by STRENGTH: the hotbar is always yours, plus 3 main slots at Strength 0, unlocking upward to all 27 as you train it. false = every vanilla slot is available from the start and never locks. TURN THIS OFF for compatibility with any mod that rearranges the player inventory (Petite Inventory, backpack/slot mods): both of you are laying claim to the same slot indices and the mod that owns the layout should win. Strength keeps training either way, so turning this back on resumes where you are rather than at zero.
